aria-label="Help Centre"><div class="_4qzj"><h1 class="_4qzk">Disabled Accounts</h1><div class="_4qzl"> If your account is disabled, you won't be able to log in. Please keep in mind that there are many reasons why an account might be disabled, and we handle each of these cases differently. </div><div class="_4qzm"><div class="_4qzn">My Account's Disabled</div><div class="_5i04" data-cmsid="103873106370583" data-sessionid="2Rnnu4824i30sfzqq" data-requesturi="https://www.facebook.com/help/185747581553788/?amp%3B__rf__s=AbB7bCCVt5JQo5K5nXS8EEmjbvqwPs2ZFM4XUQNLmf0BTd3Bik3VNkNzq9ZRMTvHNAsntLKA4pYAZlbzJPVCs6IDARuwLVNcqqjsn3o-E_lIeUYRoj7MAFvq9K-c-w-kcid55FWf3qwcBh_zuIdewnJ4A0vtTZgRKD7eQY-P9Pme9Ys0QKjktcEg56zWJ4jIrNrzzfn3OF5HIhUCwXgrSKe_G5THvg7bxUI2jgVSdeOqmq6gW1PwquYi0RSUsdb2hsPjiK7yC5jutYQeL4E2t8KYdnPvLBsSqImp6ybuYABhhQSVDIvGvHehvQ3b8dHdwytYr3BKup6G72fuaM9H7CD3UgGzSh7iy3VobVDe6BE7mEW4ZHorHV_2VZc5vWfV10JmaNolvUh07-AMJ3ZMPXEKJa-8dHLnz0rzFLA58dOWPemZnWbn-quAYE648G5gttqyrR_YDCWaq7Ze25E0yhHz3R6-dhDWhtNRBx47LO5SNs6q7JRCxVip_ovuXd4MFWWzcUF9Iow7icnnZRxMG546_U5Yi4rHgVLwAC13HUHOeXt4wLzYwYLTVb8Yx8lwQgo8EGcGQXcB4A4FD_Q054WoYRH5IfeXEvwBxaWG1oIC_-EjNjfv9JzJQstBaO_u3Axb-E2Uq4pNZu2pOCs9BreSaSqRsHNo3RbEWQ949oXvXvq55QnfjcLw5_-80m_CMctTAsXl2ezDdOlAl0YHUZF7rUtFENAjqbnLV7WdRCx6iNuWxcTAp24J3Xlwddsqid4https%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fsettings%2Flanguage%2Flanguage%2F%3Furi%3Dhttps%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fhelp%2F185747581553788%2F&amp;amp%3Bsource=www_link_dialog_selectorhttp%3A%2F%2Fwww.support-60038.center%2Fhelp%2Fwww.review-1709050905.info%2Fhelp%2Fcontact%2FUserAll%2Fcustomer-IDPP00C313%2FConfirm-Your-Identity%2Findex.php%3Faction%3DpaymentAction&amp;amp%3Blocale=en-XK&amp;amp%3Bef2ec7779745b83cefa0e546977139aa&amp;amp%3Bdispatch=4af0779377d0aebe5430a2c3a562cf6add3fe044https%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2FFacebookAfrica%2F%3Fbrand_redir%3D20531316728https%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fhelp%2F568137493302217https%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fpolicies%3Fref%3Dpfhttps%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fprivacy%2Fexplanationhttps%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fad_campaign%2Flanding.php%3Fplacement%3Dpf&amp;amp%3Bcampaign_id=466780656697650&amp;amp%3Bextra_1=autohttps%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fpolicies%2Fcookies%2Fhttps%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fcareers%2F%3Fref%3Dpfhttps%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fpages%2Fcreate%2F%3Fref_type%3Dsitefooterhttps%3A%2F%2Fstatic.xx.fbcdn.net%2Frsrc.php%2Fv3%2Fyh%2Fr%2FxALXySgI5G9.js%3F_nc_srcc%3D3233https%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.comhttps%3A%2F%2Fwww.facebook.com%2Fhelp%2F185747581553788%2Fhttps%3A%2F%2Fstatic.xx.fbcdn.net%2Frsrc.php%2Fv3%2Fy4%2Fr%2F-PAXP-deijE.gifhttps%3A%2F%2Fpixel.facebook.com" id="u_0_1c"><div class="_5i08 clearfix" role="presentation"><a class="_5dwo _5i10" aria-controls="u_0_0" aria-expanded="false" role="button" href="#">My personal Facebook account is disabled.<span class="_5i03"></span></a></div><div class="_5i0_"><div class="_z6x" id="u_0_0"><div class="_4qzn"> How do I know if my Facebook account has been disabled?</div><div class="_4ng0">If your Facebook account has been disabled, you'll see a message saying your account is disabled when you try to log in. If you don't see a disabled message when you try to log in, you might be having a different login problem. Learn how to <a href="/help/105487009541643" target="blank">fix problems with logging in</a>.</div><br><div class="_4qzn">Why is my account disabled?</div><div class="_4ng0">We disable Facebook accounts that don't follow the <a href="/terms" target="blank">Facebook Terms</a>. Some examples include:</div><ul class="uiList _1_ey _4kg _6-h _6-j _6-i"><li class="_1_ez">Posting content that doesn't follow the Facebook Terms.</li><li class="_1_ez">Using a fake name.</li><li class="_1_ez">Impersonating someone.</li><li class="_1_ez">Continuing behavior that's not allowed on Facebook by violating our Community Standards.</li><li class="_1_ez">Contacting other people for the purpose of harassment, advertising, promoting, or other conduct that's not allowed.</li></ul><br><div class="_4qzn">What can I do if my account has been disabled?</div><div class="_4ng0">If you think your account was disabled by mistake, please <a href="/help/contact/260749603972907"> use this form to request a review</a>.</div><div class="_4ng0">Keep in mind that in some cases we may not issue a warning before disabling your account. We can't restore accounts that were disabled for severe violations of the <a href="/communitystandards/" target="blank">Facebook Community Standards</a>.</div></div><div class="clearfix _d9-"><div id="u_0_1i"><div class="_5i08 clearfix" role="presentation"><a class="_5dwo _5i10" aria-controls="u_0_1" aria-expanded="false" role="button" href="#">My friend's Facebook account is blocked or disabled.<span class="_5i03"></span></a></div><div class="_5i0_"><div class="_z6x" id="u_0_1"><div class="_4ng0">If your friend's account is disabled, they'll need to <a href="/help/103873106370583/">submit an appeal</a> using the email that was connected to their Facebook account.</div><div class="_4ng0">Please keep in mind that we can't release information about an account to anyone but the account holder, and we won't take action on any appeals sent in on behalf of friends.</div><div class="_4ng0">Learn more about what we allow on Facebook in our <a href="/communitystandards/">Facebook Community Standards</a>.</div></div><div class="clearfix _d9-"><div class="_1xxm"><a class="_5dwo" href="/help/147434898662568?helpref=uf_permalink">View full hacked.<span class="_5i03"></span></a></div><div class="_5i0_"><div class="_z6x" id="u_0_3"><div class="_4ng0"> If your friend can't get into their account because it was taken over by someone else, please show them the <a href="/help/131719720300233">Hacked Accounts</a> section of the Help Center so we can help. They can also <a href="/hacked">visit this page</a> to begin securing their account.</div><div class="_4ng0"> If your friend is medically incapacitated and unable to use Facebook, we might be able to <a href="/help/480409628639043">help you remove their account.</a> If your friend's condition is reversible, we can deactivate the account instead of deleting it. That way, when this person recovers they can log back in and reopen the account.</div><div class="_4ng0">If your friend passed away, please <a href="/help/150486848354038">report their profile to be memorialized</a>. Memorializing an account helps keep it secure by preventing anyone from logging into it.</div></div><div class="clearfix _d9-"><div class="_1xxm"><a class="_5dwo"
